```python
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    total_end_debit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    total_end_credit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))

    open_diff: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    period_diff: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    end_diff: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))

    is_balanced: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    voucher_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    entry_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    unbalanced_subjects: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)

    check_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    check_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    check_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    trigger_source: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="MANUAL", server_default="MANUAL")
    is_blocking_close: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=True, server_default="TRUE")
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        # 条件式：仅在 is_balanced = TRUE 时强制合计相等
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = FALSE OR total_open_debit = total_open_credit",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_open"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = FALSE OR total_period_debit = total_period_credit",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_period"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = FALSE OR total_end_debit = total_end_credit",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_end"),
        # 差额字段始终自洽
        CheckConstraint("period_diff = total_period_debit - total_period_credit",
                        name="ck_m17_trial_balance_diff_period"),
        # 平衡标记与差额始终自洽
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = (open_diff = 0.00 AND period_diff = 0.00 "
            "AND end_diff = 0.00)",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_flag"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = TRUE OR unbalanced_subjects IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_unbalanced_detail"),
        Index("uk_m17_trial_balance_round", "period", "round_no", "org_id",
              unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_trial_balance_unbalanced", "period", "check_at",
              postgresql_where=text("is_balanced = FALSE")),
    )
```

```python
# app/models/m17/period.py
"""M17-05 总账与期末结账 —— 结账/年结/审计日志 ORM 模型"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional

from sqlalchemy import (
    BigInteger, Boolean, CheckConstraint, Index, Integer, Numeric, SmallInteger,
    String, TIMESTAMP, text,
)
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import JSONB
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.models.base import Base


class M17PeriodClose(Base):
    """m17_period_close（期末结账表）。

    关键约束：status 非 CLOSING 时必须 m16_notified = TRUE ——
    已结账必须已回调 M16 锁账，否则已结账期间的收入仍可被改写。
    """

    __tablename__ = "m17_period_close"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    period_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="CLOSING", server_default="CLOSING")
    fiscal_year: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    period_month: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)

    total_debit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    total_credit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    voucher_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    entry_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    subject_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    end_balance_debit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    end_balance_credit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))

    trial_balance_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    trial_balanced: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    check_passed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    check_warn_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    check_fail_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))

    closing_voucher_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    closing_voucher_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    next_period: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=True)
    open_balance_carried: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    carried_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)

    closed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)
    closed_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    closed_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)

    # ---------- M16 锁账回调（严重财务事故防线） ----------
    m16_notified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE",
        doc="是否已发 m17.period.closed 事件回调 M16 锁账")
    m16_notified_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)
    m16_notify_attempts: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    m16_notify_error: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    m16_event_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    m22_notified: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")

    # ---------- 反结账 ----------
    reopen_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    reopened_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)
    reopen_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    reopen_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    reopened_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    reopen_voucher_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    lock_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)

    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"),
        onupdate=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("total_debit = total_credit",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_balance"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status IN ('CLOSING','CLOSED','REOPENED','LOCKED')",
            name="ck_m17_period_close_status"),
        CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR trial_balanced = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_trial"),
        CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR check_passed = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_check"),
        # ★ 严重财务事故防线
        CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR m16_notified = TRUE",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_m16_notified"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "m16_notified = FALSE "
            "OR (m16_notified_at IS NOT NULL AND m16_event_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m17_period_close_m16_at"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "status = 'CLOSING' "
            "OR (closing_voucher_no IS NOT NULL AND closing_voucher_id IS NOT NULL)",
            name="ck_m17_period_close_voucher"),
        Index("uk_m17_period_close_period", "period", "org_id", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_period_close_latest", "org_id", "period"),
        Index("idx_m17_period_close_unnotified", "closed_at",
              postgresql_where=text("m16_notified = FALSE")),
    )


class M17PeriodCloseCheck(Base):
    """m17_period_close_check（结账检查项表）。"""

    __tablename__ = "m17_period_close_check"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    period_close_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=False)
    period: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    check_code: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(48), nullable=False)
    check_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(128), nullable=False)
    check_category: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="COMPLETENESS",
        server_default="COMPLETENESS")
    severity: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False, default="FAIL", server_default="FAIL")
    check_result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(8), nullable=False, default="PASS", server_default="PASS")
    check_value: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(256), nullable=True)
    expected_value: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(256), nullable=True)
    diff_value: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=True)
    detail_json: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    problem_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    check_sql: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024), nullable=True)
    check_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    check_cost_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)

    is_overridden: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    override_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    override_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    override_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)
    override_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)

    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_code IN ("
            "'VOUCHER_UNPOSTED','VOUCHER_DRAFT','VOUCHER_ENTRY_MISMATCH',"
            "'TRIAL_BALANCE','BALANCE_DIRECTION_CHECK','GENERAL_VS_DETAIL',"
            "'LEDGER_VOUCHER_MATCH','OPEN_BALANCE_CHECK','CASH_NEGATIVE',"
            "'PREVIOUS_PERIOD_CLOSED','BANK_UNRECONCILED','DEPT_PROFIT_CHECK')",
            name="ck_m17_period_close_check_code"),
        CheckConstraint("check_result IN ('PASS','WARN','FAIL')",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_check_result"),
        CheckConstraint("severity IN ('FAIL','WARN')",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_check_severity"),
        CheckConstraint("severity = 'WARN' OR check_result <> 'WARN'",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_check_severity_match"),
        # FAIL 级不可人工豁免（平衡类检查不可绕过）
        CheckConstraint("severity <> 'FAIL' OR is_overridden = FALSE",
                        name="ck_m17_period_close_check_no_override_fail"),
        # 平衡类检查项通过时差额必须为 0.00（等价 debit_total = credit_total）
        CheckConstraint(
            "check_code NOT IN ('TRIAL_BALANCE','GENERAL_VS_DETAIL',"
            "'LEDGER_VOUCHER_MATCH','OPEN_BALANCE_CHECK','DEPT_PROFIT_CHECK') "
            "OR check_result <> 'PASS' OR diff_value IS NULL "
            "OR diff_value = 0.00",
            name="ck_m17_period_close_check_diff_zero"),
        Index("uk_m17_period_close_check", "period_close_id", "check_code",
              unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_period_close_check_failed", "period", "check_at",
              postgresql_where=text("check_result IN ('FAIL','WARN')")),
    )


class M17YearClose(Base):
    """m17_year_close（年度结账表）。"""

    __tablename__ = "m17_year_close"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    fiscal_year: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(SmallInteger, nullable=False)
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="CLOSING", server_default="CLOSING")
    period_start: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    period_end: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=False)
    closed_period_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))

    total_debit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    total_credit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    voucher_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    profit_income_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    profit_expense_total: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"))
    net_profit: Mapped[Decimal] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False, default=Decimal("0.00"),
        server_default=text("0.00"), doc="本年净利润 = 收入 − 费用")

    profit_carried: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    profit_carry_voucher_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    undistributed_carried: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    undistributed_voucher_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(32), nullable=True)
    open_balance_generated: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    next_year: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=True)

    closed_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)
    closed_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    closed_by_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    locked_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)

    reopen_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        SmallInteger, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    reopen_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    reopen_approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    reopened_by: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=True)
    reopened_at: Mapped[Optional[datetime]] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=True)
    audit_report_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True)
    remark: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(1024), nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    version: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, server_default=text("0"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"),
        onupdate=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint("total_debit = total_credit",
                        name="ck_m17_year_close_balance"),
        CheckConstraint("status IN ('CLOSING','CLOSED','REOPENED','LOCKED')",
                        name="ck_m17_year_close_status"),
        CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR closed_period_count = 12",
                        name="ck_m17_year_close_all_periods"),
        CheckConstraint("net_profit = profit_income_total - profit_expense_total",
                        name="ck_m17_year_close_net_profit"),
        CheckConstraint("period_start = fiscal_year::text || '-01'",
                        name="ck_m17_year_close_period_start"),
        CheckConstraint("period_end = fiscal_year::text || '-12'",
                        name="ck_m17_year_close_period_end"),
        Index("uk_m17_year_close_year", "fiscal_year", "org_id", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_year_close_latest", "org_id", "fiscal_year"),
    )


class M17LedgerLog(Base):
    """m17_ledger_log（账簿操作审计日志表）。只增不改，按月分区。"""

    __tablename__ = "m17_ledger_log"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(BigInteger, primary_key=True)
    operated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, primary_key=True,
        server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"), doc="操作时间，分区键")
    log_no: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    trace_id: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=False)
    action_group: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="VOUCHER", server_default="VOUCHER")
    period: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(7), nullable=True)
    voucher_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    voucher_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    subject_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    from_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    to_status: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    debit_total: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=True)
    credit_total: Mapped[Optional[Decimal]] = mapped_column(
        Numeric(18, 2), nullable=True)
    changed_fields: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    before_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    after_snapshot: Mapped[Optional[Dict[str, Any]]] = mapped_column(
        JSONB, nullable=True)
    change_summary: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(1024), nullable=True)
    change_reason: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(
        String(512), nullable=True)
    is_sensitive: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    is_financial_impact: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False, server_default="FALSE")
    approval_no: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    operator_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(BigInteger, nullable=True)
    operator_name: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    operator_role: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(32), nullable=True)
    operator_ip: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(64), nullable=True)
    client_type: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(16), nullable=True)
    result: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(16), nullable=False, default="SUCCESS", server_default="SUCCESS")
    error_code: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(8), nullable=True)
    error_msg: Mapped[Optional[str]] = mapped_column(String(512), nullable=True)
    affected_rows: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    duration_ms: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=True)
    org_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        BigInteger, nullable=False, default=1, server_default=text("1"))
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        TIMESTAMP, nullable=False, server_default=text("CURRENT_TIMESTAMP"))

    __table_args__ = (
        CheckConstraint(r"log_no ~ '^LL[0-9]{16}$'",
                        name="ck_m17_ledger_log_no_format"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action IN ("
            "'CREATE','SUBMIT','AUDIT','REJECT','POST','REVERSE',"
            "'CANCEL','CLOSE','REOPEN','YEAR_CLOSE','YEAR_REOPEN',"
            "'TRIAL_BALANCE','BALANCE_ADJUST','CARRY_FORWARD',"
            "'VIEW_SENSITIVE','EXPORT','PRINT','PARTITION_MAINTAIN')",
            name="ck_m17_ledger_log_action"),
        CheckConstraint("result IN ('SUCCESS','FAILED','REJECTED')",
                        name="ck_m17_ledger_log_result"),
        CheckConstraint(
            "action NOT IN ('REOPEN','YEAR_REOPEN') OR approval_no IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m17_ledger_log_reopen_approval"),
        # 凭证类操作记录的借贷快照必然平衡
        CheckConstraint(
            "action NOT IN ('SUBMIT','AUDIT','POST','REVERSE','CANCEL') "
            "OR (debit_total IS NOT NULL AND credit_total IS NOT NULL "
            "AND debit_total = credit_total)",
            name="ck_m17_ledger_log_balance"),
        Index("uk_m17_ledger_log_no", "log_no", "operated_at", unique=True),
        Index("idx_m17_ledger_log_voucher", "voucher_no",
              text("operated_at DESC")),
        Index("idx_m17_ledger_log_financial", "period",
              text("operated_at DESC"),
              postgresql_where=text("is_financial_impact = TRUE")),
        {
            "postgresql_partition_by": "RANGE (operated_at)",
            "comment": "账簿审计日志：只增不改，按月分区保留 36 个月",
        },
    )


# 模型与表对照
# | 模型类            | 表名                      | 含义             |
# |-------------------|---------------------------|------------------|
# | M17Voucher        | m17_voucher               | 会计凭证主表      |
# | M17VoucherEntry   | m17_voucher_entry         | 凭证明细分录      |
# | M17LedgerDetail   | m17_ledger_detail         | 明细账           |
# | M17LedgerBalance  | m17_ledger_balance        | 科目余额表        |
# | M17LedgerGeneral  | m17_ledger_general        | 总账汇总         |
# | M17TrialBalance   | m17_trial_balance         | 试算平衡         |
# | M17PeriodClose    | m17_period_close          | 期末结账         |
# | M17PeriodCloseCheck | m17_period_close_check  | 结账检查项        |
# | M17YearClose      | m17_year_close            | 年度结账         |
# | M17LedgerLog      | m17_ledger_log            | 账簿审计日志      |
```

**模型与表对照**

| 模型类 | 表名 | 含义 |
|---|---|---|
| `M17Voucher` | `m17_voucher` | 会计凭证主表（借贷平衡 CHECK + 已过账不可改） |
| `M17VoucherEntry` | `m17_voucher_entry` | 凭证明细分录（只增不改） |
| `M17LedgerDetail` | `m17_ledger_detail` | 明细账（只增不改） |
| `M17LedgerBalance` | `m17_ledger_balance` | 科目余额表（期初+发生=期末，借贷两方向） |
| `M17LedgerGeneral` | `m17_ledger_general` | 总账汇总（含总账明细核对标记） |
| `M17TrialBalance` | `m17_trial_balance` | 试算平衡（条件式合计校验） |
| `M17PeriodClose` | `m17_period_close` | 期末结账（含 M16 锁账回调状态） |
| `M17PeriodCloseCheck` | `m17_period_close_check` | 结账检查项（12 项） |
| `M17YearClose` | `m17_year_close` | 年度结账（净利润 CHECK） |
| `M17LedgerLog` | `m17_ledger_log` | 账簿审计日志（按月分区，只增不改） |

---

### 4.13 Pydantic Schema 定义

```python
# app/schemas/m17/ledger.py
"""M17-05 总账与期末结账 —— Pydantic V2 Schema"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Literal, Optional

from pydantic import (
    BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator, model_validator,
)

# ==================== 枚举字面量 ====================

DcFlag = Literal["D", "C"]
BalanceDirection = Literal["DEBIT", "CREDIT"]
SubjectCategory = Literal[
    "ASSET", "LIABILITY", "EQUITY", "COST", "PL_INCOME", "PL_EXPENSE",
]
VoucherStatus = Literal[
    "DRAFT", "SUBMITTED", "AUDITED", "POSTED", "REVERSED", "CANCELLED",
]
VoucherSource = Literal[
    "REVENUE", "COST", "DEPT", "BANK", "MANUAL", "CLOSING", "REVERSAL",
]
VoucherWord = Literal["记", "收", "付", "转"]
AuxType = Literal[
    "DEPT", "STAFF", "PATIENT", "PROJECT", "SUPPLIER", "BIZ_LINE", "NONE",
]
PeriodStatus = Literal["NOT_OPEN", "OPEN", "CLOSED", "LOCKED", "REOPENED"]
CloseStatus = Literal["CLOSING", "CLOSED", "REOPENED", "LOCKED"]
CheckResult = Literal["PASS", "WARN", "FAIL"]
CheckSeverity = Literal["FAIL", "WARN"]
ReversalType = Literal["RED", "BLUE"]

PERIOD_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[0-9]{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$")
VOUCHER_NO_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^R?V[0-9]{6}-[0-9]{4}$")
SUBJECT_CODE_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[0-9]{4}([0-9]{2}){0,3}$")
AMOUNT_MAX = Decimal("10000000000.00")


# ==================== 凭证明细 ====================

class VoucherEntryIn(BaseModel):
    """凭证明细分录入参（单条分录）。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True)

    entry_no: int = Field(..., ge=1, le=999, description="分录行号，从 1 起")
    subject_code: str = Field(..., max_length=32, description="会计科目编码（末级）")
    dc_flag: DcFlag = Field(..., description="借贷方向 D 借 / C 贷")
    amount: Decimal = Field(..., gt=0, le=AMOUNT_MAX,
                            description="分录金额（元，2 位小数），恒为正")
    aux_type: Optional[AuxType] = Field(None, description="辅助核算类型")
    aux_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="辅助核算编码")
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="科室 ID")
    biz_line: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=24, description="收入业务线")
    entry_summary: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="分录摘要")
    cash_flow_item: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                          description="现金流量项目编码")

    @field_validator("subject_code")
    @classmethod
    def check_subject(cls, v: str) -> str:
        """科目编码格式校验。"""
        if not SUBJECT_CODE_PATTERN.match(v):
            raise ValueError("科目编码必须为 4 位一级编码，可含 2 位二级/三级扩展")
        return v

    @field_validator("amount")
    @classmethod
    def check_amount(cls, v: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """金额必须精确到分（最多 2 位小数）且为正。"""
        if v != v.quantize(Decimal("0.01")):
            raise ValueError("金额必须精确到分（最多 2 位小数）")
        if v <= 0:
            raise ValueError("分录金额必须大于 0.00；贷方用 dc_flag='C' 表达，不用负数")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_aux(self) -> "VoucherEntryIn":
        """辅助核算字段成对与科室一致性。"""
        if (self.aux_type is None) != (self.aux_code is None):
            raise ValueError("辅助核算类型与编码必须成对出现")
        if self.aux_type == "DEPT" and not self.dept_id:
            raise ValueError("辅助核算为科室（DEPT）时必须填写 dept_id")
        return self


class VoucherCreateIn(BaseModel):
    """新建凭证入参（Header + Entries 一次性提交）。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True, extra="forbid")

    period: str = Field(..., max_length=7, description="会计期间 YYYY-MM")
    voucher_date: date = Field(..., description="凭证日期，须落在期间内")
    voucher_word: VoucherWord = Field("记", description="凭证字")
    voucher_source: VoucherSource = Field("MANUAL", description="凭证来源")
    summary: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=512, description="凭证摘要")
    source_bill_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64, description="来源业务单号")
    attachment_count: int = Field(0, ge=0, le=999, description="附件张数")
    entries: List[VoucherEntryIn] = Field(
        ..., min_length=2, max_length=999, description="分录列表，至少 2 条")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")

    @field_validator("period")
    @classmethod
    def check_period(cls, v: str) -> str:
        """期间格式校验。"""
        if not PERIOD_PATTERN.match(v):
            raise ValueError("会计期间格式必须为 YYYY-MM（月份 01~12）")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_balance_and_date(self) -> "VoucherCreateIn":
        """【核心校验】借贷平衡与日期归属。"""
        if self.voucher_date.strftime("%Y-%m") != self.period:
            raise ValueError(
                "凭证日期 %s 不属于会计期间 %s，业务日不能跨期"
                % (self.voucher_date.isoformat(), self.period))
        dr = sum((e.amount for e in self.entries if e.dc_flag == "D"), Decimal("0.00"))
        cr = sum((e.amount for e in self.entries if e.dc_flag == "C"), Decimal("0.00"))
        if dr != cr:
            raise ValueError(
                "借贷不平衡：借方合计 %.2f，贷方合计 %.2f，差额 %.2f"
                % (dr, cr, dr - cr))
        if dr == 0:
            raise ValueError("凭证借贷合计不能为 0.00")
        nos = [e.entry_no for e in self.entries]
        if len(nos) != len(set(nos)):
            raise ValueError("分录行号不能重复")
        if set(nos) != set(range(1, len(nos) + 1)):
            raise ValueError("分录行号必须从 1 连续编号")
        return self


class VoucherUpdateIn(BaseModel):
    """更新凭证入参（仅 DRAFT 状态可用）。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    voucher_date: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="凭证日期")
    summary: Optional[str] = Field(None, min_length=1, max_length=512,
                                   description="凭证摘要")
    attachment_count: Optional[int] = Field(None, ge=0, le=999, description="附件张数")
    entries: Optional[List[VoucherEntryIn]] = Field(
        None, min_length=2, max_length=999, description="分录列表（全量替换）")
    remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=1024, description="备注")
    version: int = Field(..., ge=0, description="乐观锁版本号")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_entries_balance(self) -> "VoucherUpdateIn":
        """若提交分录，必须借贷平衡。"""
        if self.entries:
            dr = sum((e.amount for e in self.entries if e.dc_flag == "D"),
                     Decimal("0.00"))
            cr = sum((e.amount for e in self.entries if e.dc_flag == "C"),
                     Decimal("0.00"))
            if dr != cr:
                raise ValueError(
                    "借贷不平衡：借方合计 %.2f，贷方合计 %.2f" % (dr, cr))
        return self


class VoucherReversalIn(BaseModel):
    """红字冲销入参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True, extra="forbid")

    reason: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=512,
                        description="冲销原因，至少 5 字")
    reversal_type: ReversalType = Field(
        "RED", description="RED 红字全额冲销 / BLUE 蓝字差额更正")
    reversal_date: Optional[date] = Field(
        None, description="冲销凭证日期，缺省为当前期间最后一天")
    blue_amount: Optional[Decimal] = Field(
        None, gt=0, description="蓝字更正金额（reversal_type=BLUE 时必填）")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_blue(self) -> "VoucherReversalIn":
        """蓝字更正必须给金额。"""
        if self.reversal_type == "BLUE" and self.blue_amount is None:
            raise ValueError("蓝字更正（BLUE）必须填写 blue_amount")
        if self.reversal_type == "RED" and self.blue_amount is not None:
            raise ValueError("红字全额冲销（RED）不需要 blue_amount")
        return self


class VoucherAuditIn(BaseModel):
    """凭证审核入参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    approved: bool = Field(..., description="审核通过则 True，驳回则 False")
    audit_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="审核意见")
    reject_reason: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, min_length=5, max_length=512, description="驳回原因（驳回时必填）")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_reject(self) -> "VoucherAuditIn":
        """驳回必须填原因。"""
        if not self.approved and not self.reject_reason:
            raise ValueError("驳回凭证必须填写 reject_reason（至少 5 字）")
        return self


class VoucherPostIn(BaseModel):
    """凭证过账入参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    voucher_ids: List[int] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                    description="批量过账的凭证 ID 列表")
    voucher_nos: List[str] = Field(default_factory=list,
                                    description="批量过账的凭证号列表")
    post_all_audited: bool = Field(
        False, description="是否过账本期全部已审核凭证")
    check_balance: bool = Field(True, description="过账前是否校验明细合计")
    continue_on_error: bool = Field(False, description="遇错是否继续（默认整批回滚）")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_scope(self) -> "VoucherPostIn":
        """必须指定过账范围。"""
        if not (self.voucher_ids or self.voucher_nos or self.post_all_audited):
            raise ValueError("必须指定 voucher_ids / voucher_nos / post_all_audited 之一")
        return self

class PeriodCloseIn(BaseModel):
    """执行结账入参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(str_strip_whitespace=True, extra="forbid")

    period: str = Field(..., max_length=7, description="会计期间 YYYY-MM")
    confirm: bool = Field(..., description="必须显式确认（二次确认）")
    close_remark: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=512, description="结账备注")
    skip_warn_checks: List[str] = Field(
        default_factory=list, description="显式豁免的 WARN 级检查项编码")
    override_approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, max_length=64, description="豁免审批单号（有豁免项时必填）")

    @field_validator("period")
    @classmethod
    def check_period(cls, v: str) -> str:
        """期间格式校验。"""
        if not PERIOD_PATTERN.match(v):
            raise ValueError("会计期间格式必须为 YYYY-MM")
        return v

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_confirm(self) -> "PeriodCloseIn":
        """必须显式确认；有豁免项必须有审批单号。"""
        if not self.confirm:
            raise ValueError("结账是不可逆操作，必须显式确认 confirm = true")
        if self.skip_warn_checks and not self.override_approval_no:
            raise ValueError("豁免 WARN 级检查项必须提供 override_approval_no")
        return self

# ==================== 查询 ====================

class VoucherQuery(BaseModel):
    """凭证列表查询条件。"""

    period: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=7, description="会计期间")
    period_from: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=7, description="期间起")
    period_to: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=7, description="期间止")
    voucher_date_from: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="凭证日期起")
    voucher_date_to: Optional[date] = Field(None, description="凭证日期止")
    status: Optional[List[VoucherStatus]] = Field(None, description="状态多选")
    voucher_source: Optional[List[VoucherSource]] = Field(None, description="来源多选")
    voucher_word: Optional[List[VoucherWord]] = Field(None, description="凭证字多选")
    subject_code: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=32,
                                        description="包含该科目的凭证")
    keyword: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=64,
                                   description="凭证号/摘要/来源单号模糊检索")
    maker_id: Optional[int] = Field(None, gt=0, description="制单人")
    is_reversal: Optional[bool] = Field(None, description="是否冲销凭证")
    amount_min: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, description="金额下限（元）")
    amount_max: Optional[Decimal] = Field(None, ge=0, description="金额上限（元）")
    pending_audit: bool = Field(False, description="仅看待审核")
    pending_post: bool = Field(False, description="仅看待过账")
    page: int = Field(1, ge=1, description="页码")
    page_size: int = Field(20, ge=1, le=200, description="每页条数")
    order_by: Literal["voucher_date", "voucher_no", "debit_total"] = Field(
        "voucher_no", description="排序字段")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_range(self) -> "VoucherQuery":
        """日期与金额区间校验。"""
        if self.voucher_date_from and self.voucher_date_to:
            if self.voucher_date_from > self.voucher_date_to:
                raise ValueError("凭证日期起不得晚于日期止")
        if self.amount_min is not None and self.amount_max is not None:
            if self.amount_min > self.amount_max:
                raise ValueError("金额下限不得大于上限")
        return self


class LedgerQuery(BaseModel):

    show_zero: bool = Field(False, description="是否显示零余额行")
    page: int = Field(1, ge=1, description="页码")
    page_size: int = Field(50, ge=1, le=500, description="每页条数")

    end_balance: Decimal = Field(..., description="期末余额（元）")
    ytd_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本年累计借方（元）")
    ytd_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本年累计贷方（元）")
    is_contra: bool = Field(False, description="是否备抵科目")


class TrialBalanceOut(BaseModel):
    """试算平衡出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    period: str = Field(..., description="会计期间")
    round_no: int = Field(..., description="试算轮次")
    is_balanced: bool = Field(..., description="是否平衡")
    subject_count: int = Field(..., description="参与试算科目数")
    total_open_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="期初借方余额合计（元）")
    total_open_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="期初贷方余额合计（元）")
    total_period_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期借方发生额合计（元）")
    total_period_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期贷方发生额合计（元）")
    total_end_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="期末借方余额合计（元）")
    total_end_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="期末贷方余额合计（元）")
    open_diff: Decimal = Field(..., description="期初差额（元）")
    period_diff: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期发生差额（元）")
    end_diff: Decimal = Field(..., description="期末差额（元）")
    voucher_count: int = Field(..., description="参与试算凭证数")
    entry_count: int = Field(..., description="参与试算分录数")
    unbalanced_subjects: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = Field(
        None, description="不平衡科目明细")
    check_at: datetime = Field(..., description="试算时间")


class PeriodCloseOut(BaseModel):
    """期末结账出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    period: str = Field(..., description="会计期间")
    period_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="期间编号 PE+YYYYMM")
    status: CloseStatus = Field(..., description="结账状态")
    status_text: str = Field(..., description="状态中文")
    fiscal_year: int = Field(..., description="会计年度")
    period_month: int = Field(..., description="期间月份")
    total_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期借方发生额合计（元）")
    total_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期贷方发生额合计（元）")
    voucher_count: int = Field(..., description="本期凭证数")
    trial_balanced: bool = Field(..., description="试算是否平衡")
    check_passed: bool = Field(..., description="检查项是否全部通过")
    closing_voucher_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="结账凭证号")
    next_period: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="下期期间")
    closed_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="结账时间")
    closed_by_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="结账人")
    # ---------- M16 锁账回调状态 ----------
    m16_notified: bool = Field(..., description="是否已回调 M16 锁账")
    m16_notified_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="回调成功时间")
    m16_event_id: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="事件 ID")
    m16_sync_status: str = Field(
        ..., description="回调状态 SYNCED 已同步 / PENDING 待投递 / FAILED 失败")
    reopen_count: int = Field(0, description="反结账次数")
    reopen_reason: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="反结账原因")
    reopen_approval_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="反结账审批单号")


class YearCloseOut(BaseModel):
    """年度结账出参。"""

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    fiscal_year: int = Field(..., description="会计年度")
    status: CloseStatus = Field(..., description="年结状态")
    status_text: str = Field(..., description="状态中文")
    period_start: str = Field(..., description="起始期间")
    period_end: str = Field(..., description="结束期间")
    closed_period_count: int = Field(..., description="已结账期间数（须为 12）")
    total_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="全年借方发生额合计（元）")
    total_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="全年贷方发生额合计（元）")
    profit_income_total: Decimal = Field(..., description="全年损益收入合计（元）")
    profit_expense_total: Decimal = Field(..., description="全年损益费用合计（元）")
    net_profit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本年净利润（元），负数为亏损")
    profit_carry_voucher_no: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="损益结转凭证号")
    undistributed_voucher_no: Optional[str] = Field(
        None, description="未分配利润结转凭证号")
    next_year: Optional[int] = Field(None, description="次年年度")
    closed_at: Optional[datetime] = Field(None, description="年结时间")
    closed_by_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="年结人")
    reopen_count: int = Field(0, description="反年结次数")


class SubjectBalanceOut(BaseModel):
    """P-09 科目余额契约出参（供 M16、M22 调用）。"""

    subject_code: str = Field(..., description="科目编码")
    subject_name: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="科目名称")
    period: str = Field(..., description="会计期间")
    balance_direction: BalanceDirection = Field(..., description="余额方向")
    open_balance: Decimal = Field(..., description="期初余额（元）")
    period_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期借方发生额（元）")
    period_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本期贷方发生额（元）")
    end_balance: Decimal = Field(..., description="期末余额（元）")
    ytd_debit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本年累计借方发生额（元）")
    ytd_credit: Decimal = Field(..., description="本年累计贷方发生额（元）")
    period_status: PeriodStatus = Field(..., description="会计期间状态")
    is_period_closed: bool = Field(..., description="期间是否已结账（M16 锁账依据）")
    query_time: datetime = Field(..., description="查询时间")


VoucherCreateIn.model_rebuild()
VoucherDetailOut.model_rebuild()
PeriodClosePreviewOut.model_rebuild()
```

**Schema 与用途对照**

| Schema | 用途 | 对应接口 |
|---|---|---|
| `VoucherCreateIn` | 新建凭证入参（含借贷平衡校验） | API-08 |
| `VoucherUpdateIn` | 更新凭证（仅 DRAFT） | API-09 |
| `VoucherReversalIn` | 红字冲销入参 | API-13 |
| `VoucherAuditIn` | 凭证审核入参 | API-10 |
| `VoucherPostIn` | 批量过账入参 | API-11 |
| `VoucherQuery` | 凭证列表查询 | API-04 |
| `LedgerQuery` | 明细账查询 | API-19 |
| `BalanceQuery` | 科目余额表查询 | API-21 |
| `PeriodCloseIn` | 执行结账入参 | API-25 |
| `PeriodReopenIn` | 反结账入参 | API-27 |
| `YearCloseIn` | 年度结账入参 | API-29 |
| `YearReopenIn` | 反年结入参 | API-30 |
| `VoucherBriefOut` | 凭证列表出参 | API-04 |
| `VoucherDetailOut` | 凭证详情出参 | API-05 |
| `LedgerDetailOut` | 明细账行出参 | API-19 |
| `BalanceRowOut` | 余额表行出参 | API-21 |
| `TrialBalanceOut` | 试算平衡出参 | API-23 |
| `PeriodClosePreviewOut` | 结账预检出参 | API-24 |
| `PeriodCloseOut` | 结账结果出参 | API-25 |
| `YearCloseOut` | 年结结果出参 | API-29 |
| `SubjectBalanceOut` | **P-09 科目余额契约出参** | API-33 |

**三个核心校验在 Schema 层的落地：**

| 校验 | Schema 位置 | 与数据库约束的关系 |
|---|---|---|
| 借贷平衡 | `VoucherCreateIn.check_balance_and_date` | Schema 提前拦截给友好提示；数据库 `ck_m17_voucher_balance` 兜底 |
| 日期归属期间 | `VoucherCreateIn.check_balance_and_date` | Schema 提前拦截；数据库 `ck_m17_voucher_date_in_period` 兜底 |
| 金额精度到分 | `VoucherEntryIn.check_amount` | Schema 提前拦截；数据库 `NUMERIC(18,2)` 精度兜底 |
| 分录行号连续 | `VoucherCreateIn.check_balance_and_date` | Schema 校验；数据库 `uk_m17_voucher_entry_no` 保唯一 |
| 结账二次确认 | `PeriodCloseIn.check_confirm` | 仅 Schema 层（无数据库对应，属操作流程要求） |
| 豁免必须审批 | `PeriodCloseIn.check_confirm` | Schema + 数据库 `ck_m17_period_close_check_override` 双保险 |

## 附录 A：表清单速查

| 表名 | 中文名 | 主键 | 关键唯一键 | 分区 | 量级（年） |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m17_voucher` | 会计凭证主表 | `(id, period)` | `(voucher_no, period)` | **是**（`period` 月分区 + DEFAULT） | 6 万 |
| `m17_voucher_entry` | 凭证明细分录表 | `(id, period)` | `(voucher_id, entry_no, period)` | **是**（`period` 月分区 + DEFAULT） | 42 万 |
| `m17_ledger_detail` | 明细账表 | `(id, period)` | `(entry_id, period)` | **是**（`period` 月分区 + DEFAULT） | 42 万 |
| `m17_ledger_balance` | 科目余额表 | `id` | `(subject_code, period, aux_type, aux_code, org_id)` | 否 | 3.6 万 |
| `m17_ledger_general` | 总账汇总表 | `id` | `(subject_code, period, org_id)` | 否 | 6 千 |
| `m17_trial_balance` | 试算平衡表 | `id` | `(period, round_no, org_id)` | 否 | 12~36 |
| `m17_period_close` | 期末结账表 | `id` | `(period, org_id)` | 否 | 12 |
| `m17_period_close_check` | 结账检查项表 | `id` | `(period_close_id, check_code)` | 否 | 144 |
| `m17_year_close` | 年度结账表 | `id` | `(fiscal_year, org_id)` | 否 | 1 |
| `m17_ledger_log` | 账簿操作审计日志表 | `(id, operated_at)` | `(log_no, operated_at)` | **是**（`operated_at` 月分区 + DEFAULT） | 48 万 |

**合计：10 张表**（3 张分区表，均含 DEFAULT 分区）

**四条会计铁律的强制位置速查：**

| 铁律 | 强制约束 / 语句 | 所在表 | 错误码 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. 凭证借贷必相等 | `ck_m17_voucher_balance CHECK (debit_total = credit_total)` | `m17_voucher` | `"1705001"` |
| 2. 明细合计 = 凭证合计 | `trg_m17_voucher_entry_total`（延期约束触发器） | `m17_voucher_entry` | `"1705002"` |
| 3. 期末 = 期初 + 本期发生 | `ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit` / `ck_m17_ledger_balance_credit` | `m17_ledger_balance` | `"1705010"` |
| 4. 已过账不可改 | `REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON m17_voucher FROM app_user` + 触发器 | `m17_voucher` / `m17_voucher_entry` | `"1705005"` |
| 5. 结账必须回调 M16 锁账 | `ck_m17_period_close_m16_notified` | `m17_period_close` | `"1705024"` |

**表间引用关系（逻辑外键，不建物理约束）：**

| 从表 | 字段 | 到表 | 基数 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m17_voucher_entry` | `voucher_id` + `period` | `m17_voucher` | N:1 | 分录属于凭证 |
| `m17_voucher` | `reverse_of_voucher_id` | `m17_voucher` | 1:0..1 | 红冲链（自关联） |
| `m17_ledger_detail` | `entry_id` + `period` | `m17_voucher_entry` | 1:1 | 过账时逐条生成 |
| `m17_ledger_detail` | `voucher_id` + `period` | `m17_voucher` | N:1 | 账证核对 |
| `m17_ledger_balance` | `subject_code` | M17-01 `m17_account_subject` | N:1 | 科目定义 |
| `m17_ledger_general` | `subject_code` | `m17_ledger_balance` | N:1（汇总） | 一级科目汇总 |
| `m17_period_close` | `period` | M17-01 `m17_account_period` | 1:1 | 期间定义 |
| `m17_period_close` | `closing_voucher_id` | `m17_voucher` | 1:0..1 | 结账凭证 |
| `m17_period_close` | `trial_balance_id` | `m17_trial_balance` | 1:0..1 | 结账前试算 |
| `m17_period_close_check` | `period_close_id` | `m17_period_close` | N:1 | 检查项 |
| `m17_year_close` | `fiscal_year` | `m17_period_close` | 1:12 | 年度含 12 期 |
| `m17_ledger_log` | `voucher_id` | `m17_voucher` | N:1 | 操作留痕（弱引用） |

## 附录 B：状态枚举汇总

**B.1 凭证状态（`voucher.status`，与共享规范 13 一字不差）**

| 取值 | 中文 | 可否修改 | 可否删除 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `DRAFT` | 草稿 | 是 | 可作废 | 制单中，可自由改分录 |
| `SUBMITTED` | 待审核 | 否 | 可作废 | 已提交，需审核或驳回 |
| `AUDITED` | 已审核 | 否 | 不可 | 待过账 |
| `POSTED` | 已过账 | **物理禁止** | **物理禁止** | 只能红冲 |
| `REVERSED` | 已冲销 | **物理禁止** | **物理禁止** | 已被红字凭证冲销 |
| `CANCELLED` | 已作废 | 否 | 不可 | 未过账即作废，必填原因 |

**B.2 凭证来源（`voucher_source`）**

| 取值 | 中文 | 生成方 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `REVENUE` | 收入 | M17-02 | 从 M16 日结生成 |
| `COST` | 成本 | M17-03 | 从 M14/M15 成本生成 |
| `DEPT` | 科室 | M17-04 | 科室核算与分摊 |
| `BANK` | 银行 | M17-06 | 银行流水与手续费 |
| `MANUAL` | 手工 | 财务手工录入 | 调账、冲正、特殊业务 |
| `CLOSING` | 结账 | M17-05 | 损益结转凭证 |
| `REVERSAL` | 冲销 | M17-05 | 红字冲销凭证 |

**B.3 会计期间状态（`period.status`，与共享规范 13 一致）**

| 取值 | 中文 | 允许录凭证 | 允许过账 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `NOT_OPEN` | 未开启 | 否 | 否 | 仅可配置 |
| `OPEN` | 已开启 | 是 | 是 | 全部业务 |
| `CLOSED` | 已结账 | 否 | 否 | 只读，更正须反结账 |
| `LOCKED` | 已封存 | 否 | 否 | 年结后，反年结需院长审批 |
| `REOPENED` | 已重开 | 是 | 是 | 反结账后临时状态 |

**B.4 结账状态（`m17_period_close.status`）**

| 取值 | 中文 | `m16_notified` 要求 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CLOSING` | 结账中 | 可为 FALSE（等待回调） | 事务进行中或回调重试中 |
| `CLOSED` | 已结账 | **必须 TRUE** | 结账完成，M16 已锁账 |
| `REOPENED` | 已反结账 | **必须 TRUE** | 期间重开，M16 已同步开放 |
| `LOCKED` | 已封存 | **必须 TRUE** | 年结完成，永久封存 |

**B.5 借贷方向（`dc_flag`）与余额方向（`balance_direction`）**

| `dc_flag` | 中文 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|
| `D` | 借 | 字段存单字母 `D`，展示层翻译为「借」 |
| `C` | 贷 | 字段存单字母 `C`，展示层翻译为「贷」 |

| `balance_direction` | 中文 | 科目类别 | 期末余额公式 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `DEBIT` | 借方余额 | 资产 / 成本 / 损益费用 | `end = open + period_debit − period_credit` |
| `CREDIT` | 贷方余额 | 负债 / 所有者权益 / 损益收入 | `end = open + period_credit − period_debit` |

**B.6 结账检查项（12 项）**

| 编码 | 名称 | 类别 | 级别 | 判定口径 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `VOUCHER_UNPOSTED` | 存在未过账凭证 | `COMPLETENESS` | `FAIL` | 未过账凭证数 = 0 |
| `VOUCHER_DRAFT` | 存在未提交草稿 | `COMPLETENESS` | `WARN` | 草稿凭证数 = 0 |
| `VOUCHER_ENTRY_MISMATCH` | 明细合计与凭证头不符 | `CONSISTENCY` | `FAIL` | 明细汇总 = 凭证头合计 |
| `TRIAL_BALANCE` | 试算不平衡 | `BALANCE` | `FAIL` | 期末借方余额合计 = 贷方余额合计 |
| `BALANCE_DIRECTION_CHECK` | 存在反向余额 | `BALANCE` | `WARN` | `end_balance >= 0` 的行占比 |
| `GENERAL_VS_DETAIL` | 总账与明细账不符 | `CONSISTENCY` | `FAIL` | `end_balance = leaf_balance_sum` |
| `LEDGER_VOUCHER_MATCH` | 账证不符 | `CONSISTENCY` | `FAIL` | 明细账发生额 = 凭证分录汇总 |
| `OPEN_BALANCE_CHECK` | 期初与上期期末不符 | `CONSISTENCY` | `FAIL` | 本期期初 = 上期期末 |
| `CASH_NEGATIVE` | 现金/存款出现贷方余额 | `BALANCE` | `FAIL` | `1001`/`1002` 期末余额 ≥ 0.00 |
| `PREVIOUS_PERIOD_CLOSED` | 上期未结账 | `COMPLIANCE` | `FAIL` | 上期状态 ∈ {`CLOSED`,`LOCKED`} |
| `BANK_UNRECONCILED` | 银行未达账项未处理 | `COMPLIANCE` | `WARN` | 未达账项数 = 0 |
| `DEPT_PROFIT_CHECK` | 科室损益与总账不符 | `CONSISTENCY` | `WARN` | 科室损益合计 = 总账损益发生额 |

**B.7 审计日志操作类型（18 种）**

| 取值 | 中文 | 分组 | 影响账务 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `CREATE` | 新建凭证 | `VOUCHER` | 否 |
| `SUBMIT` | 提交凭证 | `VOUCHER` | 否 |
| `AUDIT` | 审核通过 | `VOUCHER` | 否 |
| `REJECT` | 审核驳回 | `VOUCHER` | 否 |
| `POST` | 过账 | `VOUCHER` | **是** |
| `REVERSE` | 红字冲销 | `VOUCHER` | **是** |
| `CANCEL` | 凭证作废 | `VOUCHER` | 否 |
| `CLOSE` | 期末结账 | `CLOSE` | **是** |
| `REOPEN` | 反结账 | `CLOSE` | **是** |
| `YEAR_CLOSE` | 年度结账 | `CLOSE` | **是** |
| `YEAR_REOPEN` | 反年结 | `CLOSE` | **是** |
| `TRIAL_BALANCE` | 试算平衡 | `LEDGER` | 否 |
| `BALANCE_ADJUST` | 余额调整 | `ADJUST` | **是** |
| `CARRY_FORWARD` | 余额结转 | `ADJUST` | **是** |
| `VIEW_SENSITIVE` | 查看敏感数据 | `AUDIT` | 否 |
| `EXPORT` | 导出账簿 | `EXPORT` | 否 |
| `PRINT` | 打印凭证/账簿 | `EXPORT` | 否 |
| `PARTITION_MAINTAIN` | 分区维护 | `LEDGER` | 否 |

---

## 附录 C：关键索引与查询

**C.1 凭证列表查询（P02，最高频）**

```sql
SELECT v.voucher_no, v.period, v.voucher_date, v.voucher_word,
       v.voucher_source, v.summary, v.debit_total, v.credit_total,
       v.entry_count, v.status, v.maker_name, v.auditor_name, v.poster_name
FROM m17_voucher v
WHERE v.period = '2026-10'
  AND v.status IN ('SUBMITTED', 'AUDITED')
ORDER BY v.voucher_date, v.voucher_no
LIMIT 20;
-- 命中：idx_m17_voucher_period_status（分区裁剪到 p202610 单分区）
-- 期望：P95 < 30ms
```

**C.2 待审核清单（P03）**

```sql
SELECT voucher_no, voucher_date, summary, debit_total, maker_name, maker_at
FROM m17_voucher
WHERE status = 'SUBMITTED'
ORDER BY maker_at
LIMIT 50;
-- 命中：idx_m17_voucher_pending_audit（部分索引，只含待审核行）
-- 期望：P95 < 15ms
```

**C.3 凭证详情（含分录）**

```sql
SELECT v.voucher_no, v.summary, v.debit_total, v.credit_total, v.status,
       e.entry_no, e.subject_code, e.subject_name, e.dc_flag, e.amount,
       e.aux_type, e.aux_code, e.entry_summary
FROM m17_voucher v
JOIN m17_voucher_entry e
  ON e.voucher_id = v.id AND e.period = v.period
WHERE v.voucher_no = 'V202610-0001' AND v.period = '2026-10'
ORDER BY e.entry_no;
-- 命中：uk_m17_voucher_no + idx_m17_voucher_entry_voucher
-- 期望：P95 < 12ms
```

**C.4 过账前批量校验（明细合计 vs 凭证头）**

```sql
SELECT v.voucher_no, v.debit_total, v.credit_total, e.dr, e.cr
FROM m17_voucher v
JOIN (
    SELECT voucher_id, period,
           SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'D' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS dr,
           SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'C' THEN amount ELSE 0 END) AS cr
    FROM m17_voucher_entry
    WHERE period = '2026-10'
    GROUP BY voucher_id, period
) e ON e.voucher_id = v.id AND e.period = v.period
WHERE v.period = '2026-10'
  AND v.status = 'AUDITED'
  AND (e.dr <> v.debit_total OR e.cr <> v.credit_total);
-- 命中：idx_m17_voucher_pending_post + idx_m17_voucher_entry_subject
-- 期望：P95 < 200ms（约 5,000 张凭证 / 3.5 万条分录）
-- 返回行数必须为 0，否则抛 "1705002"
```

**C.5 明细账查询（P05）**

```sql
SELECT voucher_date, voucher_no, entry_no, summary, subject_code,
       counter_subject, debit_amount, credit_amount,
       balance_direction, running_balance
FROM m17_ledger_detail
WHERE period = '2026-10'
  AND subject_code = '1002'
ORDER BY voucher_date, voucher_no, entry_no;
-- 命中：idx_m17_ledger_detail_subject（分区裁剪到单分区）
-- 期望：P95 < 40ms（单科目约 300 行）
```

**C.6 科目余额表（P06）**

```sql
SELECT subject_code, subject_name, subject_category, balance_direction,
       open_balance, period_debit, period_credit, end_balance,
       ytd_debit, ytd_credit
FROM m17_ledger_balance
WHERE period = '2026-10'
  AND subject_category IN ('ASSET', 'LIABILITY')
  AND (end_balance <> 0.00 OR open_balance <> 0.00
       OR period_debit <> 0.00 OR period_credit <> 0.00)
ORDER BY subject_code;
-- 命中：idx_m17_ledger_balance_period
-- 期望：P95 < 50ms（约 1,800 行）
```

**C.7 试算平衡计算（P07）**

```sql
SELECT
    SUM(CASE WHEN balance_direction = 'DEBIT'  THEN open_balance  ELSE 0 END) AS dr_open,
    SUM(CASE WHEN balance_direction = 'CREDIT' THEN open_balance  ELSE 0 END) AS cr_open,
    SUM(period_debit)  AS dr_period,
    SUM(period_credit) AS cr_period,
    SUM(CASE WHEN balance_direction = 'DEBIT'  THEN end_balance ELSE 0 END) AS dr_end,
    SUM(CASE WHEN balance_direction = 'CREDIT' THEN end_balance ELSE 0 END) AS cr_end,
    COUNT(*) AS subject_cnt
FROM m17_ledger_balance
WHERE period = '2026-10';
-- 命中：idx_m17_ledger_balance_period
-- 期望：P95 < 60ms
-- 三组 dr = cr 全部成立 → is_balanced = TRUE
```

**C.8 结账前置检查组合查询（P08）**

```sql
-- ① 未过账凭证（必须为 0）
SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher
WHERE period = '2026-10' AND status IN ('SUBMITTED', 'AUDITED');
-- 命中：idx_m17_voucher_period_status

-- ② 草稿凭证（WARN）
SELECT count(*) FROM m17_voucher
WHERE period = '2026-10' AND status = 'DRAFT';

-- ③ 账证不符（明细账发生额 vs 凭证分录汇总，必须为 0 行）
SELECT d.subject_code, d.amt AS ledger_amt, e.amt AS voucher_amt
FROM (
    SELECT subject_code,
           SUM(debit_amount) - SUM(credit_amount) AS amt
    FROM m17_ledger_detail
    WHERE period = '2026-10'
    GROUP BY subject_code
) d
FULL JOIN (
    SELECT subject_code,
           SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'D' THEN amount ELSE -amount END) AS amt
    FROM m17_voucher_entry
    WHERE period = '2026-10'
    GROUP BY subject_code
) e ON e.subject_code = d.subject_code
WHERE COALESCE(d.amt, 0) <> COALESCE(e.amt, 0);
-- 命中：idx_m17_voucher_entry_subject + idx_m17_ledger_detail_subject

-- ④ 总账 vs 明细账（必须为 TRUE）
SELECT count(*) FILTER (WHERE is_balanced = FALSE) AS unbalanced
FROM m17_ledger_general WHERE period = '2026-10';
-- 命中：idx_m17_ledger_general_unbalanced

-- ⑤ 现金/存款贷方余额（必须为 0）
SELECT subject_code, end_balance FROM m17_ledger_balance
WHERE period = '2026-10'
  AND subject_code IN ('1001', '1002')
  AND balance_direction = 'DEBIT'
  AND end_balance < 0.00;

-- ⑥ 上期是否已结账
SELECT period, status FROM m17_period_close
WHERE period = '2026-09' AND org_id = 1;
-- 命中：uk_m17_period_close_period
```

**C.9 未回调 M16 的结账清单（定时重试任务）**

```sql
SELECT period, status, closed_at, m16_notify_attempts, m16_notify_error
FROM m17_period_close
WHERE m16_notified = FALSE
  AND status <> 'CLOSING'
ORDER BY closed_at;
-- 期望：0 行。命中 idx_m17_period_close_unnotified
-- 任何非空结果都是 P1 级告警：存在已结账但 M16 未锁账的期间
```

**C.10 敏感操作审计（合规）**

```sql
SELECT log_no, operated_at, action, period, voucher_no,
       change_reason, approval_no, operator_name, result
FROM m17_ledger_log
WHERE is_sensitive = TRUE
  AND operated_at >= CURRENT_TIMESTAMP - INTERVAL '90 days'
ORDER BY operated_at DESC;
-- 命中：idx_m17_ledger_log_sensitive（部分索引）
-- 期望：P95 < 60ms（只扫近期分区）
```

**C.11 账务影响操作清单（审计重点）**

```sql
SELECT period, action, count(*) AS cnt,
       SUM(debit_total) AS amount_total
FROM m17_ledger_log
WHERE is_financial_impact = TRUE
  AND period = '2026-10'
GROUP BY period, action
ORDER BY action;
-- 命中：idx_m17_ledger_log_financial
-- 期望：P95 < 40ms
```

**C.12 P-09 科目余额契约查询（供 M16/M22）**

```sql
SELECT b.subject_code, b.subject_name, b.balance_direction,
       b.open_balance, b.period_debit, b.period_credit, b.end_balance,
       b.ytd_debit, b.ytd_credit,
       pc.status AS period_status
FROM m17_ledger_balance b
LEFT JOIN m17_period_close pc
       ON pc.period = b.period AND pc.org_id = b.org_id
WHERE b.subject_code = '400101'
  AND b.period = '2026-10'
  AND b.org_id = 1;
-- 命中：uk_m17_ledger_balance_subject（无辅助核算时 aux_type='NONE'）
-- 期望：P95 < 100ms（含期间状态联查，走缓存）
```

**C.13 红冲链路追溯**

```sql
-- 查某张凭证是否已被冲销，以及冲销凭证号
SELECT v.voucher_no, v.status, r.voucher_no AS reversal_no, r.posted_at
FROM m17_voucher v
LEFT JOIN m17_voucher r
       ON r.reverse_of_voucher_id = v.id AND r.period = v.period
WHERE v.voucher_no = 'V202610-0001' AND v.period = '2026-10';
-- 命中：idx_m17_voucher_reversal
-- 期望：P95 < 10ms
```

## 附录 D：配置参数与字典

**D.1 系统参数**

| 参数键 | 默认值 | 类型 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `M17.LEDGER.VOUCHER_NO_CACHE` | 200 | int | 凭证号 Redis 预分配块大小 |
| `M17.LEDGER.AMOUNT_PRECISION` | 2 | int | 金额小数位数（固定 2，不可改） |
| `M17.LEDGER.AMOUNT_MAX` | 10000000000.00 | decimal | 单笔分录金额上限（元） |
| `M17.LEDGER.MAX_ENTRY_PER_VOUCHER` | 999 | int | 单张凭证最大分录数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.POST_BATCH_SIZE` | 500 | int | 批量过账单批凭证数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.POST_REQUIRE_AUDIT` | true | bool | 过账前必须审核（禁止跳过审核） |
| `M17.LEDGER.SEPARATION_OF_DUTY` | true | bool | 制单/审核/过账必须不同人（职责分离） |
| `M17.LEDGER.REVERSAL_REASON_MIN_LEN` | 5 | int | 冲销原因最小字数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.ALLOW_BACKDATED` | false | bool | 是否允许补录以前期间凭证（默认禁止） |
| `M17.LEDGER.BACKDATED_MAX_DAYS` | 0 | int | 补录最多允许回溯天数 |
| `M17.CLOSE.REQUIRE_TRIAL_BALANCE` | true | bool | 结账前必须试算平衡 |
| `M17.CLOSE.REQUIRE_ALL_CHECKS` | true | bool | 结账前必须通过全部 FAIL 级检查 |
| `M17.CLOSE.REQUIRE_NO_UNPOSTED` | true | bool | 结账前必须无未过账凭证 |
| `M17.CLOSE.WARN_OVERRIDE_NEED_APPROVAL` | true | bool | 豁免 WARN 检查项必须审批 |
| `M17.CLOSE.M16_NOTIFY_RETRY_MINUTES` | 5 | int | M16 锁账回调重试间隔（分钟） |
| `M17.CLOSE.M16_NOTIFY_MAX_ATTEMPTS` | 288 | int | 回调最大尝试次数（24 小时） |
| `M17.CLOSE.M16_NOTIFY_ALERT_AFTER` | 3 | int | 尝试 N 次后升级告警 |
| `M17.CLOSE.REOPEN_NEED_APPROVAL` | true | bool | 反结账必须审批 |
| `M17.CLOSE.YEAR_REOPEN_NEED_DIRECTOR` | true | bool | 反年结必须院长审批 |
| `M17.CLOSE.REOPEN_REASON_MIN_LEN` | 5 | int | 反结账原因最小字数 |
| `M17.CLOSE.YEAR_REOPEN_REASON_MIN_LEN` | 10 | int | 反年结原因最小字数 |
| `M17.CLOSE.AUTO_CLOSE_IMMUTABLE_DAYS` | 30 | int | 结账后 N 天自动禁止反结账 |
| `M17.LEDGER.PARTITION_PREBUILD_MONTHS` | 3 | int | 预建未来几个月分区 |
| `M17.LEDGER.PARTITION_PREBUILD_DAY` | 25 | int | 每月第几天预建下月分区 |
| `M17.LEDGER.LOG_RETENTION_MONTHS` | 36 | int | 审计日志在线保留月数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.CACHE_BALANCE_SECONDS` | 120 | int | 科目余额缓存秒数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.CACHE_TRIAL_SECONDS` | 60 | int | 试算平衡缓存秒数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.EXPORT_MAX_ROWS` | 100000 | int | 单次导出最大行数 |
| `M17.LEDGER.SENSITIVE_GRANT_HOURS` | 4 | int | 敏感数据查看授权时长 |

**D.2 字典项**

| 字典编码 | 名称 | 取值 |
|---|---|---|
| `M17_VOUCHER_STATUS` | 凭证状态 | `DRAFT` / `SUBMITTED` / `AUDITED` / `POSTED` / `REVERSED` / `CANCELLED` |
| `M17_VOUCHER_SOURCE` | 凭证来源 | `REVENUE` / `COST` / `DEPT` / `BANK` / `MANUAL` / `CLOSING` / `REVERSAL` |
| `M17_VOUCHER_WORD` | 凭证字 | `记` / `收` / `付` / `转` |
| `M17_DC_FLAG` | 借贷方向 | `D` 借 / `C` 贷 |
| `M17_BALANCE_DIRECTION` | 余额方向 | `DEBIT` 借 / `CREDIT` 贷 |
| `M17_SUBJECT_CATEGORY` | 科目类别 | `ASSET` / `LIABILITY` / `EQUITY` / `COST` / `PL_INCOME` / `PL_EXPENSE` |
| `M17_AUX_TYPE` | 辅助核算类型 | `DEPT` / `STAFF` / `PATIENT` / `PROJECT` / `SUPPLIER` / `BIZ_LINE` / `NONE` |
| `M17_PERIOD_STATUS` | 期间状态 | `NOT_OPEN` / `OPEN` / `CLOSED` / `LOCKED` / `REOPENED` |
| `M17_CLOSE_STATUS` | 结账状态 | `CLOSING` / `CLOSED` / `REOPENED` / `LOCKED` |
| `M17_REVERSAL_TYPE` | 冲销方式 | `RED` 红字全额 / `BLUE` 蓝字差额 |
| `M17_CHECK_RESULT` | 检查结果 | `PASS` / `WARN` / `FAIL` |
| `M17_CHECK_SEVERITY` | 检查级别 | `FAIL`（阻断）/ `WARN`（告警） |
| `M17_BIZ_LINE` | 收入业务线 | `OUTPATIENT` / `INPATIENT` / `DAY_SURGERY` / `OPTICAL` / `REFRACTIVE` / `EXAM` / `LAB` / `PHARMACY` / `OTHER` |
| `M17_LOG_ACTION` | 审计操作类型 | 见附录 B.7（18 种） |

**D.3 标准会计科目（本子模块常引用）**

| 科目编码 | 科目名称 | 类别 | 余额方向 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `1001` | 库存现金 | `ASSET` | `DEBIT` | 现金收入 |
| `1002` | 银行存款 | `ASSET` | `DEBIT` | 银行账户 |
| `1122` | 应收账款 | `ASSET` | `DEBIT` | 医保应收 |
| `1405` | 库存商品 | `ASSET` | `DEBIT` | 药品/耗材存货 |
| `1601` | 固定资产 | `ASSET` | `DEBIT` | 设备 |
| `1602` | 累计折旧 | `ASSET` | `CREDIT` | **备抵科目**（`is_contra = TRUE`） |
| `2202` | 应付账款 | `LIABILITY` | `CREDIT` | 供应商 |
| `2211` | 应付职工薪酬 | `LIABILITY` | `CREDIT` | 人力成本 |
| `2221` | 应交税费 | `LIABILITY` | `CREDIT` | — |
| `3103` | 本年利润 | `EQUITY` | `CREDIT` | 年结结转目标 |
| `3104` | 利润分配-未分配利润 | `EQUITY` | `CREDIT` | 年结最终去处 |
| `4001` | 医疗收入 | `PL_INCOME` | `CREDIT` | 医疗服务收入 |
| `4002` | 药品收入 | `PL_INCOME` | `CREDIT` | — |
| `4003` | 其他收入 | `PL_INCOME` | `CREDIT` | — |
| `5001` | 医疗业务成本 | `PL_EXPENSE` | `DEBIT` | 药品/耗材成本 |
| `5101` | 管理费用 | `PL_EXPENSE` | `DEBIT` | 行政 |
| `5401` | 其他业务支出 | `PL_EXPENSE` | `DEBIT` | — |
| `6602` | 财务费用 | `PL_EXPENSE` | `DEBIT` | 手续费、利息 |
