## 附录 E：建表依赖顺序与 Alembic 迁移

**E.1 外部依赖（必须先于本子模块存在）**

| 顺序 | 表/对象 | 所属模块 | 使用方式 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `m17_account_subject` | M17-01 | 科目编码与类别（只读引用） |
| 2 | `m17_account_period` | M17-01 | 会计期间定义与期初余额（只读引用） |
| 3 | `m17_account_voucher_word` | M17-01 | 凭证字配置（只读引用） |
| 4 | `mdm_dept` | M01-01 | 辅助核算-科室（只读引用） |
| 5 | PostgreSQL 扩展 `pg_trgm` | PG 16 | 摘要模糊检索的 GIN 索引 |
| 6 | 角色 `app_user` | DBA | 接收 `REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE` 的对象 |

**E.2 本子模块建表顺序**

```
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ① m17_voucher（分区表）    │  无内部依赖
        │   凭证主表                │  含 DEFAULT 分区
        └────────────┬─────────────┘
                     │ 1:N
                     ▼
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ② m17_voucher_entry      │  依赖 ①
        │   （分区表，含 DEFAULT）    │  含延期约束触发器
        └────────────┬─────────────┘
                     │ 过账时逐条写入
                     ▼
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ③ m17_ledger_detail      │  依赖 ②
        │   （分区表，含 DEFAULT）    │
        └────────────┬─────────────┘
                     │ 按科目+期间汇总
                     ▼
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ④ m17_ledger_balance     │  依赖 ③（含铁律 3 两条 CHECK）
        └────────────┬─────────────┘
                     │ 一级科目汇总
                     ▼
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ⑤ m17_ledger_general     │  依赖 ④
        └──────────────────────────┘

  独立表（无内部依赖）：
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ⑥ m17_trial_balance      │  试算平衡
        └──────────────────────────┘
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ⑦ m17_period_close       │  依赖 ⑤⑥ 与 ①（结账凭证）
        └────────────┬─────────────┘
                     │ 1:N
                     ▼
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ⑧ m17_period_close_check │  依赖 ⑦
        └──────────────────────────┘
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ⑨ m17_year_close         │  依赖 ⑦（12 期）
        └──────────────────────────┘
        ┌──────────────────────────┐
        │ ⑩ m17_ledger_log         │  审计日志（分区表，含 DEFAULT）
        └──────────────────────────┘
```

**建表顺序（严格）：**

```
1. m17_voucher               （凭证主表，分区表 + DEFAULT 分区）
2. m17_voucher_entry         （分录，分区表 + DEFAULT 分区 + 延期触发器）
3. m17_ledger_detail         （明细账，分区表 + DEFAULT 分区）
4. m17_ledger_balance        （余额表，铁律 3 两条 CHECK）
5. m17_ledger_general        （总账汇总）
6. m17_trial_balance         （试算平衡）
7. m17_period_close          （期末结账，M16 回调状态约束）
8. m17_period_close_check    （结账检查项）
9. m17_year_close            （年度结账）
10. m17_ledger_log           （审计日志，分区表 + DEFAULT 分区）
```

**E.3 Alembic 迁移脚本**

```python
"""M17-05 总账与期末结账 —— 建表迁移

revision = "m17_05_001"
down_revision = "m17_04_001"
"""

import sqlalchemy as sa
from alembic import op
from sqlalchemy.dialects import postgresql

revision = "m17_05_001"
down_revision = "m17_04_001"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None


def upgrade() -> None:
    """按依赖顺序建表，并强制会计恒等式约束。"""
    op.execute("CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm")

    # ==================== 1. 凭证主表（分区） ====================
    op.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE m17_voucher (
            id                      BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            period                  VARCHAR(7)    NOT NULL,
            voucher_no              VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
            voucher_word            VARCHAR(8)    NOT NULL DEFAULT '记',
            voucher_date            DATE          NOT NULL,
            voucher_source          VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MANUAL',
            source_bill_no          VARCHAR(64),
            source_batch_no         VARCHAR(64),
            attachment_count        SMALLINT      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            entry_count             SMALLINT      NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            debit_total             NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
            credit_total            NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0.00,
            status                  VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
            period_status_snap      VARCHAR(16),
            summary                 VARCHAR(512)  NOT NULL,
            maker_id                BIGINT,
            maker_name              VARCHAR(64),
            maker_at                TIMESTAMP,
            auditor_id              BIGINT,
            auditor_name            VARCHAR(64),
            audited_at              TIMESTAMP,
            poster_id               BIGINT,
            poster_name             VARCHAR(64),
            posted_at               TIMESTAMP,
            is_reversal             BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            reverse_of_voucher_id   BIGINT,
            reverse_of_voucher_no   VARCHAR(32),
            reversed_by_voucher_id  BIGINT,
            reverse_reason          VARCHAR(512),
            reversal_type           VARCHAR(16),
            cancel_reason           VARCHAR(512),
            cancelled_by            BIGINT,
            cancelled_at            TIMESTAMP,
            is_closing              BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            is_locked               BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            org_id                  BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            version                 INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
            remark                  VARCHAR(1024),
            ext_json                JSONB,
            created_by              BIGINT,
            created_at              TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            updated_by              BIGINT,
            updated_at              TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            CONSTRAINT pk_m17_voucher PRIMARY KEY (id, period),
            -- ★ 会计铁律 1：借贷必相等
            CONSTRAINT ck_m17_voucher_balance
                CHECK (debit_total = credit_total),
            CONSTRAINT ck_m17_voucher_status
                CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','SUBMITTED','AUDITED','POSTED',
                                  'REVERSED','CANCELLED')),
            CONSTRAINT ck_m17_voucher_entry_count CHECK (entry_count >= 2)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (period)
        """
    )
    # 预建分区（示例：2026-10、2026-11）+ DEFAULT 兜底
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_p202610 PARTITION OF m17_voucher "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10') TO ('2026-11')"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_p202611 PARTITION OF m17_voucher "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11') TO ('2026-12')"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_pdefault PARTITION OF m17_voucher DEFAULT"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m17_voucher_no "
        "ON m17_voucher (voucher_no, period)"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE INDEX idx_m17_voucher_period_status "
        "ON m17_voucher (period, status, voucher_date)"
    )

    # ==================== 2. 凭证明细（分区 + 延期约束触发器） ====================
    op.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_entry (
            id                 BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            period             VARCHAR(7)    NOT NULL,
            voucher_id         BIGINT        NOT NULL,
            voucher_no         VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
            entry_no           SMALLINT      NOT NULL,
            subject_code       VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
            subject_name       VARCHAR(128),
            subject_level      SMALLINT,
            subject_category   VARCHAR(16),
            dc_flag            VARCHAR(1)    NOT NULL,
            amount             NUMERIC(18,2) NOT NULL,
            currency           VARCHAR(8)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CNY',
            original_amount    NUMERIC(18,2),
            exchange_rate      NUMERIC(14,6),
            aux_type           VARCHAR(16),
            aux_code           VARCHAR(64),
            aux_name           VARCHAR(128),
            dept_id            BIGINT,
            biz_line           VARCHAR(24),
            entry_summary      VARCHAR(512),
            quantity           NUMERIC(14,2),
            unit_price         NUMERIC(14,4),
            cash_flow_item     VARCHAR(32),
            org_id             BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
            created_at         TIMESTAMP     NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            CONSTRAINT pk_m17_voucher_entry PRIMARY KEY (id, period),
            CONSTRAINT ck_m17_voucher_entry_dc_flag CHECK (dc_flag IN ('D','C')),
            CONSTRAINT ck_m17_voucher_entry_amount CHECK (amount > 0)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (period)
        """
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_entry_p202610 "
        "PARTITION OF m17_voucher_entry "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10') TO ('2026-11')"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_entry_p202611 "
        "PARTITION OF m17_voucher_entry "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-11') TO ('2026-12')"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_voucher_entry_pdefault "
        "PARTITION OF m17_voucher_entry DEFAULT"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX uk_m17_voucher_entry_no "
        "ON m17_voucher_entry (voucher_id, entry_no, period)"
    )

    # 延期约束触发器：事务提交时校验明细借贷平衡与合计一致
    op.execute(
        """
        CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_m17_voucher_entry_check_total()
        RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
        DECLARE
            v_dr NUMERIC(18,2);
            v_cr NUMERIC(18,2);
            v_head_dr NUMERIC(18,2);
            v_head_cr NUMERIC(18,2);
        BEGIN
            SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'D' THEN amount ELSE 0 END),
                   SUM(CASE WHEN dc_flag = 'C' THEN amount ELSE 0 END)
              INTO v_dr, v_cr
              FROM m17_voucher_entry
             WHERE voucher_id = COALESCE(NEW.voucher_id, OLD.voucher_id)
               AND period     = COALESCE(NEW.period, OLD.period);
            SELECT debit_total, credit_total
              INTO v_head_dr, v_head_cr
              FROM m17_voucher
             WHERE id = COALESCE(NEW.voucher_id, OLD.voucher_id)
               AND period = COALESCE(NEW.period, OLD.period);
            IF v_dr <> v_cr THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION '凭证明细借贷不平衡: 借 % 贷 %', v_dr, v_cr;
            END IF;
            IF v_dr <> v_head_dr OR v_cr <> v_head_cr THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION '明细合计 % 与凭证头合计 % 不一致', v_dr, v_head_dr;
            END IF;
            RETURN NULL;
        END;
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql
        """
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE CONSTRAINT TRIGGER trg_m17_voucher_entry_total "
        "AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON m17_voucher_entry "
        "DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED "
        "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_m17_voucher_entry_check_total()"
    )

    # ==================== 3~5. 账簿表 ====================
    op.create_table(
        "m17_ledger_detail",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("period", sa.String(7), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("voucher_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("voucher_no", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("entry_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("subject_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("dc_flag", sa.String(1), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("debit_amount", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("credit_amount", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("balance_direction", sa.String(8), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("running_balance", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("posted_at", postgresql.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=False),
    )

    op.create_table(
        "m17_ledger_balance",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("period", sa.String(7), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("subject_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("subject_category", sa.String(16), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("balance_direction", sa.String(8), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("aux_type", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="NONE"),
        sa.Column("aux_code", sa.String(64), nullable=False,
                  server_default="NONE"),
        sa.Column("open_balance", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("period_debit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("period_credit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("end_balance", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        # ★ 会计铁律 3：期末 = 期初 + 本期发生（借/贷两个方向）
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "balance_direction <> 'DEBIT' "
            "OR end_balance = open_balance + period_debit - period_credit",
            name="ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit"),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "balance_direction <> 'CREDIT' "
            "OR end_balance = open_balance + period_credit - period_debit",
            name="ck_m17_ledger_balance_credit"),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m17_ledger_balance_subject", "m17_ledger_balance",
                    ["subject_code", "period", "aux_type", "aux_code", "org_id"],
                    unique=True)

    op.create_table(
        "m17_ledger_general",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("period", sa.String(7), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("subject_code", sa.String(32), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("balance_direction", sa.String(8), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("open_balance", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("period_debit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("period_credit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("end_balance", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("leaf_balance_sum", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("is_balanced", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FALSE"),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = FALSE OR end_balance = leaf_balance_sum",
            name="ck_m17_ledger_general_balanced"),
    )

    # ==================== 6. 试算平衡 ====================
    op.create_table(
        "m17_trial_balance",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("period", sa.String(7), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("round_no", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="1"),
        sa.Column("total_period_debit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("total_period_credit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("period_diff", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("is_balanced", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FALSE"),
        sa.Column("unbalanced_subjects", postgresql.JSONB(), nullable=True),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = FALSE "
            "OR total_period_debit = total_period_credit",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_period"),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "is_balanced = TRUE OR unbalanced_subjects IS NOT NULL",
            name="ck_m17_trial_balance_unbalanced_detail"),
    )

    # ==================== 7. 期末结账 ====================
    op.create_table(
        "m17_period_close",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("period", sa.String(7), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("status", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="CLOSING"),
        sa.Column("total_debit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("total_credit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("trial_balanced", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FALSE"),
        sa.Column("check_passed", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FALSE"),
        sa.Column("m16_notified", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FALSE"),
        sa.Column("m16_notified_at", postgresql.TIMESTAMP(), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("m16_event_id", sa.String(64), nullable=True),
        sa.CheckConstraint("total_debit = total_credit",
                           name="ck_m17_period_close_balance"),
        sa.CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR trial_balanced = TRUE",
                           name="ck_m17_period_close_trial"),
        sa.CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR check_passed = TRUE",
                           name="ck_m17_period_close_check"),
        # ★ 会计铁律 5：已结账必须已回调 M16 锁账
        sa.CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR m16_notified = TRUE",
                           name="ck_m17_period_close_m16_notified"),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m17_period_close_period", "m17_period_close",
                    ["period", "org_id"], unique=True)
    op.create_index("idx_m17_period_close_unnotified", "m17_period_close",
                    ["closed_at"],
                    postgresql_where=sa.text("m16_notified = FALSE"))

    # ==================== 8. 结账检查项 ====================
    op.create_table(
        "m17_period_close_check",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("period_close_id", sa.BigInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("period", sa.String(7), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("check_code", sa.String(48), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("severity", sa.String(8), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FAIL"),
        sa.Column("check_result", sa.String(8), nullable=False,
                  server_default="PASS"),
        sa.Column("diff_value", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=True),
        sa.Column("is_overridden", sa.Boolean(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="FALSE"),
        # FAIL 级检查项不可人工豁免
        sa.CheckConstraint("severity <> 'FAIL' OR is_overridden = FALSE",
                           name="ck_m17_period_close_check_no_override_fail"),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m17_period_close_check", "m17_period_close_check",
                    ["period_close_id", "check_code"], unique=True)

    # ==================== 9. 年度结账 ====================
    op.create_table(
        "m17_year_close",
        sa.Column("id", sa.BigInteger(), sa.Identity(), primary_key=True),
        sa.Column("fiscal_year", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False),
        sa.Column("status", sa.String(16), nullable=False,
                  server_default="CLOSING"),
        sa.Column("closed_period_count", sa.SmallInteger(), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0"),
        sa.Column("total_debit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("total_credit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.Column("net_profit", sa.Numeric(18, 2), nullable=False,
                  server_default="0.00"),
        sa.CheckConstraint("total_debit = total_credit",
                           name="ck_m17_year_close_balance"),
        sa.CheckConstraint("status = 'CLOSING' OR closed_period_count = 12",
                           name="ck_m17_year_close_all_periods"),
        sa.CheckConstraint(
            "net_profit = profit_income_total - profit_expense_total",
            name="ck_m17_year_close_net_profit"),
    )
    op.create_index("uk_m17_year_close_year", "m17_year_close",
                    ["fiscal_year", "org_id"], unique=True)

    # ==================== 10. 审计日志（分区） ====================
    op.execute(
        """
        CREATE TABLE m17_ledger_log (
            id                    BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY,
            operated_at           TIMESTAMP   NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            log_no                VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            action                VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL,
            action_group          VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'VOUCHER',
            period                VARCHAR(7),
            voucher_id            BIGINT,
            voucher_no            VARCHAR(32),
            is_sensitive          BOOLEAN     NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            is_financial_impact   BOOLEAN     NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
            result                VARCHAR(16) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'SUCCESS',
            created_at            TIMESTAMP   NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
            CONSTRAINT pk_m17_ledger_log PRIMARY KEY (id, operated_at)
        ) PARTITION BY RANGE (operated_at)
        """
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_ledger_log_p202610 PARTITION OF m17_ledger_log "
        "FOR VALUES FROM ('2026-10-01') TO ('2026-11-01')"
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TABLE m17_ledger_log_pdefault PARTITION OF m17_ledger_log DEFAULT"
    )

    # ==================== 11. 凭证不可变性（铁律 4） ====================
    # 第 1 层：数据库权限 —— 应用账号对凭证表只有 INSERT / SELECT
    op.execute("REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON m17_voucher FROM app_user")
    op.execute("REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON m17_voucher_entry FROM app_user")
    op.execute("REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE ON m17_ledger_log FROM app_user")
    op.execute("GRANT INSERT, SELECT ON m17_ledger_log TO app_user")

    # 第 2 层：触发器 —— 已过账凭证禁止修改
    op.execute(
        """
        CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_m17_voucher_immutable()
        RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
        BEGIN
            IF OLD.status IN ('POSTED', 'REVERSED') THEN
                RAISE EXCEPTION
                    '凭证 % 已过账（状态 %），禁止修改；更正请使用红字冲销',
                    OLD.voucher_no, OLD.status;
            END IF;
            RETURN NEW;
        END;
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql
        """
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TRIGGER trg_m17_voucher_no_update "
        "BEFORE UPDATE ON m17_voucher "
        "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_m17_voucher_immutable()"
    )

    # 第 3 层：触发器 —— 凭证表禁止物理删除
    op.execute(
        """
        CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION trg_m17_voucher_no_delete()
        RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
        BEGIN
            RAISE EXCEPTION '凭证表禁止物理删除（凭证号 %），请使用作废或红冲',
                OLD.voucher_no;
        END;
        $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql
        """
    )
    op.execute(
        "CREATE TRIGGER trg_m17_voucher_block_delete "
        "BEFORE DELETE ON m17_voucher "
        "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_m17_voucher_no_delete()"
    )

    # ==================== 12. updated_at 触发器 ====================
    for tbl in ("m17_voucher", "m17_ledger_balance", "m17_ledger_general",
                "m17_period_close", "m17_year_close"):
        op.execute(
            "CREATE TRIGGER trg_%s_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON %s "
            "FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION trg_set_updated_at()" % (tbl, tbl)
        )


def downgrade() -> None:
    """按反向依赖顺序回滚。"""
    for tbl in ("m17_ledger_log", "m17_year_close",
                "m17_period_close_check", "m17_period_close",
                "m17_trial_balance", "m17_ledger_general",
                "m17_ledger_balance", "m17_ledger_detail",
                "m17_voucher_entry", "m17_voucher"):
        op.execute("DROP TABLE IF EXISTS %s CASCADE" % tbl)
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS trg_m17_voucher_immutable()")
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS trg_m17_voucher_no_delete()")
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS trg_m17_voucher_entry_check_total()")
```

**E.4 建表后校验清单**

| # | 校验项 | 期望结果 |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | 插入借贷不平衡凭证（借 100.00 / 贷 99.99） | 违反 `ck_m17_voucher_balance`，INSERT 失败 |
| 2 | 插入 `eye_side` 类似非法值（`period='2026-13'`） | 违反 `ck_m17_voucher_period_format` |
| 3 | `UPDATE m17_voucher SET debit_total = 1.00 WHERE status='POSTED'` | 被 `trg_m17_voucher_no_update` 拒绝 |
| 4 | `DELETE FROM m17_voucher` | 被 `trg_m17_voucher_block_delete` 拒绝 |
| 5 | `app_user` 执行 `UPDATE m17_voucher` | 权限拒绝（已 REVOKE） |
| 6 | 插入借方科目 `end_balance` 算错 | 违反 `ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit` |
| 7 | 插入贷方科目 `end_balance` 算错（用加借减贷） | 违反 `ck_m17_ledger_balance_credit` |
| 8 | 插入 `status='CLOSED'` 但 `m16_notified=FALSE` | 违反 `ck_m17_period_close_m16_notified` |
| 9 | 插入分录借贷不平（明细层） | 事务提交时被延期约束触发器拒绝 |
| 10 | 插入 `m17_voucher` 未知期间（如 `2099-01`） | 落入 `pdefault` 分区，不报错 |
| 11 | 插入 `m17_trial_balance` `is_balanced=TRUE` 但合计不等 | 违反 `ck_m17_trial_balance_period` |
| 12 | 插入 `m17_year_close` `net_profit` 不等于收入减费用 | 违反 `ck_m17_year_close_net_profit` |
| 13 | 插入 `m17_period_close_check` `severity='FAIL'` 且 `is_overridden=TRUE` | 违反 `ck_m17_period_close_check_no_override_fail` |
| 14 | `SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class WHERE relname LIKE 'm17_%_pdefault'` | 3（凭证、分录、明细账、日志共 4 个 DEFAULT 分区） |

---

## 附录 F：文档维护约定

| 约定 | 说明 |
|---|---|
| **变更入口** | 表结构以本文档为准；变更后须同步更新《详细设计》第 4 章指引块的表数（9 张改为实际值） |
| **命名不可变** | 已发布的表名、字段名不可改名（影响 M16-09 P-03、M22 P-09 的只读引用） |
| **会计恒等式不可弱化** | `ck_m17_voucher_balance`、`ck_m17_ledger_balance_debit/_credit` 任何情况下不得删除或改为较弱形式；如需调整计算口径，必须同时更新附录 A 与详细设计第 3.4 节 |
| **凭证不可变不可绕过** | `REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE` 语句不得被注释掉；新增凭证类表必须同样声明 |
| **新增凭证类表** | 若新增任何含 `debit_total` / `credit_total` 的表，必须有 `CHECK (debit_total = credit_total)` 或等价的单侧校验 |
| **新增余额类表** | 若新增任何含 `open_balance` / `end_balance` 的表，必须按余额方向写两条 CHECK |
| **金额字段** | 一律 `NUMERIC(18,2)`，命名带 `_amount` / `_amt` / `balance`；**严禁 FLOAT / DOUBLE / REAL** |
| **比率字段** | 一律 `NUMERIC(9,6)`（6 位小数，避免分摊尾差） |
| **分区表** | 新建分区表必须同时建 `DEFAULT` 分区，并在预建任务中注册 |
| **只增不改表** | `m17_voucher_entry`、`m17_ledger_detail`、`m17_ledger_log` 三类表不设 `updated_at` / `is_deleted`，须在用途中注明 |
| **枚举扩展** | 凭证状态、期间状态、结账状态的取值必须与共享规范 §13 保持一致，不得自创 |
| **敏感字段** | 新增敏感字段须登记到 `m17_ledger_log` 的 `is_sensitive` 留痕范围 |
| **文档同步** | 本文档变更须同步更新：详细设计第 4 章指引块、`M17_财务核算管理_数据结构设计汇总.md` 表清单 |

## 版本历史

| 版本 | 日期 | 修订说明 | 修订人 |
|---|---|---|---|
| V1.0 | 2026-10-31 | 首次发布：10 张表（凭证 2 + 账簿 4 + 试算 1 + 结账 3）、四条会计铁律全部落为数据库约束、3 张分区表均含 DEFAULT 分区、ORM 与 Schema 全覆盖、附录 A~F 齐全 | 系统设计组 |

**文档结束**
